| X1_30 | 22 | Correlation between country mean safety score and 2018 GDP growth (footnote 34) | 0.468 | -0.4551 | -0.4551 | DIFFERENT | Pearson correlation across countries, using the external data snapshot in reports/external/. The external series have been revised since 2020, so tolerance is 0.05. GDP growth, annual % (NY.GDP.MKTP.KD.ZG), 2018. On the footnote's scale (1 = more safe, 3 = less safe) faster growth goes with lower scores, so the correlation is negative (-0.455); its size is within 0.013 of the published 0.468. The report appears to print it without the sign. |
